# Implementation status register

Generated by `node scripts/docs/build-register.mjs` from the owner's unified master prompt (`docs/requirements/UNIFIED_MASTER_PROMPT.md`) and the statuses in `docs/register/status.json`. **Do not edit this file by hand**: change the status file and rebuild.

Each list item in a requirement section of the prompt is one row (ID = section + position). Status values are exactly **Implemented**, **Yet to do** or **Not feasible**. *Implemented* needs evidence (code and a test or a verified run); *Not feasible* rows are listed separately at the end with the reason and the way forward.

**Totals:** 1857 requirements: **1137 Implemented**, **656 Yet to do**, **64 Not feasible**.

## 4. Workflow, Security, Data Integrity and Release Non-Negotiables

| Requirement ID | Feature / Requirement | Confirmation Status | Evidence / Code Reference | Action Required | Test Evidence / Result | Blocker / Feasibility Reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| M4-01 | Enforce the workflow state machine on the server. | Implemented | server/engine/engine.ts (pure state machine) + service.ts mutate (lock + state_version) | None | tests/engine/*, integration |  |
| M4-02 | Preserve supported Sequential, Parallel, Any-One, Any-One Sequence, Selected Parallel, Any-N-of-M and supported Custom/Hybrid patterns. | Implemented | Sequential, parallel SPLIT/JOIN, ANY_ONE, ANY_N, conditions, DoA bands, custom builder | None | engine.test.ts, v140-engine.test.ts |  |
| M4-03 | Validate conditional branches, rejection, return/resubmission, reopening, cancellation, recall and parallel completion rules. | Implemented | decide/resubmit/reopen/withdraw/recall engine operations | None | v160-engine.test.ts, engine-v160.int |  |
| M4-04 | Enforce self-approval restrictions, conflict-of-interest policies and eligibility checks. | Implemented | engine decide: no self-approval; D-085 approver eligibility; delegate recheck | None | engine tests, directory-messages.test.ts |  |
| M4-05 | Validate requester, approver, delegate, consultant, watcher and administrator permissions independently. | Implemented | loadRequestView/isParticipant/visibility.ts; consultee read-only; admin role checks | None | workspace.int, engine-v160.int |  |
| M4-06 | Prevent privilege escalation, circular delegation, duplicate decisions and unauthorized workflow transitions. | Implemented | delegateTask (no circular, max 3), state_version conflict, engine authority | None | engine-v160.int concurrency test |  |
| M4-07 | Revalidate permissions and workflow state at the time of every consequential action. | Implemented | every action re-reads the session (requireApiArea) and locks the request | None | integration suites |  |
| M4-08 | Preserve immutable decision history and reconstructable approval paths. | Implemented | approval_events + audit_logs append-only triggers (D-012); request_revisions | None | approvals.int, request-detail.int |  |
| M4-09 | Record important changes with actor, action, timestamp, record reference, workflow/configuration version and relevant before/after values. | Yet to do |  | Audit rows carry actor/action/time/record; add workflow version and before/after values where missing |  |  |
| M4-10 | Never repair a workflow by silently rewriting historical decisions. | Implemented | No code path rewrites decisions; recovery tools append events | None | v140.int blocked-step recovery |  |
| M4-11 | Use safe idempotent retries and reconciliation before repeating operations with side effects. | Implemented | outbox claim + delivery lease + SLA claim rows (INSERT IGNORE) | None | jobs.int, session4.int |  |
| M4-12 | Protect reset/session tokens, secrets and sensitive information from API responses, logs and errors. | Implemented | S-01 encrypted retry payloads, log redaction, no tokens in responses | None | log-redaction.test.ts, jobs.int |  |
| M4-13 | Do not enable approval by email or WhatsApp reply unless separately approved by the business and security owners. | Implemented | No reply-to-approve endpoint exists (BD-7) | None |  |  |
| M4-14 | Do not perform destructive operations, mass record changes, irreversible data migrations, production retention deletion, backup overwrites or production deployment without explicit authorization. | Implemented | No destructive production operation performed; deletes are soft; migrations additive | None |  |  |
| M4-15 | Use feature flags or equivalent controls when appropriate, but never as a substitute for server-side authorization. | Yet to do |  | Add feature flags for new modules (Super Admin) |  |  |
| M4-16 | Keep development, testing and production configurations separated. | Implemented | config/environment.ts per environment; e2e/integration use separate DB | None |  |  |
| M4-17 | Do not claim production readiness based only on visual appearance or a successful build. | Implemented | Readiness stated from evidence only (docs/IMPLEMENTATION_REPORT.md) | None |  |  |

| Requirement ID | Feature / Requirement | Confirmation Status | Evidence / Code Reference | Action Required | Test Evidence / Result | Blocker / Feasibility Reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A7.4-01 | Pending: a valid next action is outstanding. | Implemented | semantics.ts | None | see docs/ANALYTICS_TEST_MATRIX.md |  |
| A7.4-02 | Overdue: an applicable deadline has passed. | Implemented | semantics.ts (overdue) | None | see docs/ANALYTICS_TEST_MATRIX.md |  |
| A7.4-03 | SLA breached: the applicable SLA has been violated. | Implemented | SLA BREACHED | None | see docs/ANALYTICS_TEST_MATRIX.md |  |
| A7.4-04 | Unassigned: a required assignment is missing or no eligible approver is available. | Implemented | blocked (BLOCKED steps) | None | see docs/ANALYTICS_TEST_MATRIX.md |  |
| A7.4-05 | Blocked: progress is prevented by a dependency or explicit hold. | Implemented | blocked and paused, kept apart | None | see docs/ANALYTICS_TEST_MATRIX.md |  |
| A7.4-06 | Stuck: progress has not occurred beyond a configurable operational threshold, based on reliable activity timestamps. | Implemented | stuck (Needs attention) | None | see docs/ANALYTICS_TEST_MATRIX.md |  |
| A7.4-07 | Automation failed: a background or workflow automation operation failed. | Implemented | failed job runs; messages not sent | None | see docs/ANALYTICS_TEST_MATRIX.md |  |
| A7.4-08 | Awaiting requester: the next action belongs to the requester. | Implemented | awaiting_requester | None | see docs/ANALYTICS_TEST_MATRIX.md |  |
| A7.4-10 | Paused: the workflow or SLA is explicitly paused under an authorized policy. | Implemented | paused | None | see docs/ANALYTICS_TEST_MATRIX.md |  |
| A7.4-11 | Terminal: the request has reached an approved, rejected, withdrawn or cancelled end state, as defined by the application. | Implemented | TERMINAL_STATES | None | see docs/ANALYTICS_TEST_MATRIX.md |  |

| ARequirements-11 | Define whether period filters use event time, record creation time or the latest state. | Implemented | event time (dictionary) | None | see docs/ANALYTICS_TEST_MATRIX.md |  |

| ARequirements-13 | Ensure scheduled reports execute at the intended local time. | Implemented | nextRunAt in India time | None | see docs/ANALYTICS_TEST_MATRIX.md |  |

## Not feasible (separate, with reasons)

| Requirement ID | Section | Feature / Requirement | Reason and evidence | Missing prerequisite / owner | Safe alternative or future path |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A3.4-07 | 3.4 Super Admin workspace | External integration health. | The platform has no ERP or external business integration (only email/WhatsApp providers); no transaction amounts, spend or reconciliation data exist | An ERP integration project | Provider (email/WhatsApp) health is reported under notifications |
| A3.4-08 | 3.4 Super Admin workspace | API latency, application errors and database/storage health where instrumented. | No application performance, database or storage telemetry is instrumented on GoDaddy shared hosting | Owner: add hosting monitoring (APM) or a monitoring service | Job runs, logs with request ids and /api/health |
